\displaystyle W^{(j)}({\mathcal{C}}_{M})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L_{j}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{L_{j}}{e}_{1,\left|S_{j\ell}\right|}^{\top}\left(\Sigma_{S_{j\ell}S_{j\ell}}\right)^{-1}\left({X}_{S_{j\ell}}-{\mu}_{S_{j\ell}}\right)\Big(Y-\mathrm{E}Y-\Sigma_{pS_{j\ell}}\left(\Sigma_{S_{j\ell}S_{j\ell}}\right)^{-1}\left({X}_{S_{j\ell}}-{\mu}_{S_{j\ell}}\right)\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L_{j}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{L_{j}}\frac{m_{r}}{L_{j}\sigma_{t+j\mid\left(1,\ldots,t,S_{M,j,\ell}\right)}^{2}}\left(\left(M_{j,i}-\mathrm{E}M_{j}\right)-{\beta}_{t+j\mid\left(1,\ldots,t,S_{M,j,\ell}\right)}^{\top}\left(X_{\left(1,\ldots,t,S_{M,\ell,j}\right),i}-{\mathrm{E}X}_{\left(1,\ldots,t,S_{M,\ell,j}\right)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\times\Big(Y-\mathrm{E}Y-\Sigma_{pS_{j\ell}}\left(\Sigma_{S_{j\ell}S_{j\ell}}\right)^{-1}\left({X}_{S_{j\ell}}-{\mu}_{S_{j\ell}}\right)\Big)

where
e_{i,k}
the
i
-th column of the
k\times k
identity matrix and
{\beta}_{t+j\mid\left(1,\ldots,t,S_{M,j,\ell}\right)}^{\top}:=\left(\Sigma\right)_{j\left(1,\ldots,t,S_{M,j,\ell}\right)}\penalty\left(\Sigma_{\left(1,\ldots,t,S_{M,j,\ell}\right)\left(1,\ldots,t,S_{M,j,\ell}\right)}\right)^{-1}
. Therefore, we can obtain

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\big(\widehat{{IM}}_{j}^{OLS}-\overline{IM}_{j}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\theta_{MA,j}\Big\{W_{i}^{(j)}({\mathcal{C}}_{M})+\left[\operatorname{plim}\widehat{\Gamma}_{M,(C,A)}\widehat{\Gamma}_{M,(C,A)}^{\top}\right]_{jj}^{1/2}\epsilon_{Y,i}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\Big(\overline{\xi}_{j}({\mathcal{C}}_{M})-{\beta}_{YM,j}\Big)\left[\operatorname{plim}\widehat{\Gamma}_{A,C}\widehat{\Gamma}_{A,C}^{\top}\right]^{1/2}\Big[(I_{p}-B_{MM}^{\top})^{-1}\Sigma_{M}(I_{p}-B_{MM})^{-1}\Big]_{jj}^{1/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{M,j,i}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(1)\,\rightsquigarrow\,\mathcal{N}\big(0,\sigma_{\overline{IM}_{j}}^{2}\big)

where

\displaystyle\sigma_{\overline{IM}_{j}}^{2}:=\mathrm{E}\bigg[\theta_{MA,j}\Big\{W^{(j)}({\mathcal{C}}_{M})+\left[\operatorname{plim}\widehat{\Gamma}_{M,(C,A)}\widehat{\Gamma}_{M,(C,A)}^{\top}\right]_{jj}^{1/2}\epsilon_{Y}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\Big(\overline{\xi}_{j}({\mathcal{C}}_{M})-{\beta}_{YM,j}\Big)\left[\operatorname{plim}\widehat{\Gamma}_{A,C}\widehat{\Gamma}_{A,C}^{\top}\right]^{1/2}\Big[(I_{p}-B_{MM}^{\top})^{-1}\Sigma_{M}(I_{p}-B_{MM})^{-1}\Big]_{jj}^{1/2}\varepsilon_{M,j}\bigg]^{2},

which implies
\sigma_{\overline{IM}_{j}}^{2}
can be estimated by

\displaystyle\widehat{\sigma}_{\overline{IM}_{j}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg[\widehat{\theta}_{MA,j}\Big\{\widehat{W}_{i}^{(j)}({\widehat{\mathcal{C}}}_{M})+\left[\widehat{\Gamma}_{M,(C,A)}\widehat{\Gamma}_{M,(C,A)}^{\top}\right]_{jj}^{1/2}\widehat{\epsilon}_{Y,i}\Big\}

(C.6)

\displaystyle+\Big(\widetilde{\xi}_{j}(\widehat{\mathcal{C}}_{M})-\widehat{\beta}_{YM,j}\Big)\left[\widehat{\Gamma}_{A,C}\widehat{\Gamma}_{A,C}^{\top}\right]^{1/2}\Big[(I_{p}-\widehat{B}_{MM}^{\top})^{-1}\widehat{\Sigma}_{M}(I_{p}-\widehat{B}_{MM})^{-1}\Big]_{jj}^{1/2}\widehat{\varepsilon}_{M,j,i}\bigg]^{2}

where
\widehat{B}_{MM}
can be estimated incidentally by a version of PC algorithm
(
Harris and
Drton 2013
)
. Finally, when
\theta_{MA,j}=\overline{\xi}_{j}({\mathcal{C}}_{M})-{\beta}_{YM,j}=0
, we will have
\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\mathrm{P}\big(\sqrt{n}\big|\widehat{IM}_{j}^{OLS}-IM_{j}\big|\geq\widehat{\sigma}_{\overline{IM}_{j}}\Phi^{-1}(1-\alpha/2)\big)>1-\alpha
by using the similar argument in the proof of Theorem
7.3
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
7.11
:

We will prove the consistency for
\widehat{DM}_{j}^{\text{QR}}
and semiparametric efficiency for
\widehat{TM}_{j}^{\text{avg},\,\text{QR}}
. The proof of other parts can be similarly derived. We first prove the consistency.

Consistency of

\widehat{DM}_{j}^{\text{QR}}
:

Note that
\widehat{DM}_{j}^{\text{QR}}-DM_{j}=\mathbb{P}_{n}\big\langle\widehat{S}^{\text{eff, nonpar}}\big(\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(\bm{\cdot},0,C)\big)\big\rangle
,
so it is sufficient to prove
\mathbb{P}_{n}\widehat{S}^{\text{eff, nonpar}}\big(\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\big)\,\xlongrightarrow{\mathrm{P}}\,0
for any
a^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}
. Denote
\pi_{x_{S}}^{(T)}(m_{T})=\pi_{x_{S}}(m_{T})
, Then we write

\displaystyle\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\pi_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)=\int_{\mathcal{M}}\mu(C,1,m)\pi_{C,1}(m_{j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)

\displaystyle\psi_{j}^{(1)}\big(e_{0},\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)}\big)=\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{{e}_{0}(C)}\bigg\{\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu(C,1,m_{j},M_{-j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\psi_{j}^{(2)}\big({e}_{a^{\prime}},\mu,\pi_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)=\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{{e}_{a^{\prime}}(C)}\bigg\{\int_{\mathcal{M}_{-j}}\mu(C,1,M_{j},m_{-j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{-j}-\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\psi_{j}^{(3)}\big(e_{1},\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(-j)},\pi_{C,1,M_{j}}^{(-j)},\mu\big)=\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{e_{1}(C)}\frac{\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(M_{-j})}{\pi_{C,1,M_{j}}(M_{-j})}\Big\{Y-\mu(C,1,M)\Big\}

then
\mathbb{P}_{n}S^{\text{eff, nonpar}}\big(\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\big)=\sum_{\ell=0}^{3}\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(\ell)}(\cdot)
. We break the proof into four parts,
Part
\bm{\ell}
gives the consistency under different model
\mathscr{M}_{j,\,\ell}
.

Part 0.
When model
\mathscr{M}_{0}
is correctly specified, i.e.,
\widehat{\mu}
,
\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)}
, and
\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}
are consistency. We have

\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)=\mathbb{P}_{n}\Big[\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)-\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\pi_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)\Big]+\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\pi_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big),

with
\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\pi_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)\,\xlongrightarrow{\mathrm{P}}\,P\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\pi_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)=0
. For another part, we will use empirical processes technique. Indeed, we have

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\Big[\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)-\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\pi_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)\Big]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\big\{\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)-\mu(C,1,m)\big\}\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m\bigg|

\displaystyle+\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\mu(C,1,m)\big\{\widehat{\pi}_{C,a}(m_{j})-\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\big\}\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m\bigg|

\displaystyle+\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\mu(C,1,m)\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\big\{\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})-\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\big\}\,\mathrm{d}m\bigg|.

For sufficient small constant
\varepsilon>0
, we define a set of functions
\mathcal{U}(\varepsilon)
that contains conditional expectation
\mu_{\varepsilon}
such that

\mathrm{E}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\big\{\mu_{\varepsilon}(C,1,m)-\mu(C,1,m)\big\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}m\leq\varepsilon^{2}.

then we consider

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\sup_{\mu_{\varepsilon}\,\in\,\mathcal{U}(\varepsilon)}\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\big\{\mu_{\varepsilon}(C,1,m)-\mu(C,1,m)\big\}\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\sup_{\mu_{\varepsilon}\,\in\,\mathcal{U}(\varepsilon)}\bigg[\mathbb{P}_{n}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\big|\mu_{\varepsilon}(C,1,m)-\mu(C,1,m)\big|\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{n}\sup_{\mu_{\varepsilon}\,\in\,\mathcal{U}(\varepsilon)}\mathrm{E}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\big|\mu_{\varepsilon}(C,1,m)-\mu(C,1,m)\big|\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m

\displaystyle+\mathrm{E}\sup_{\mu_{\varepsilon}\,\in\,\mathcal{U}(\varepsilon)}\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}}\big|\mu_{\varepsilon}(C,1,m)-\mu(C,1,m)\big|\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m

\displaystyle-\mathrm{E}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\big|\mu_{\varepsilon}(C,1,m)-\mu(C,1,m)\big|\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m\bigg]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{Cauchy Inequality and $\ell^{2}$ assumption}}{\leq}\varepsilon^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathrm{E}\sup_{\mu_{\varepsilon}\,\in\,\mathcal{U}(\varepsilon)}\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}}\big|\mu_{\varepsilon}(C,1,m)-\mu(C,1,m)\big|\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m

\displaystyle-\mathrm{E}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\big|\mu_{\varepsilon}(C,1,m)-\mu(C,1,m)\big|\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m\bigg]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{Corollary 5.1 in \cite[cite]{\@@bibref{Authors Phrase1YearPhrase2}{chernozhukov2014gaussian}{\@@citephrase{(}}{\@@citephrase{)}}}}}{\lesssim}\varepsilon^{2}+n^{-1/2}\sqrt{n^{\vartheta}}\varepsilon\big(\log(\varepsilon)+\log n\big)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{Let }\varepsilon\rightarrow 0\text{ with Assumption \ref{ass_fun_class}}}{\longrightarrow}0.

Therefore, we must have
\mathbb{P}_{n}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\big\{\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)-\mu(C,1,m)\big\}\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m=o_{p}(1)
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{n}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\mu(C,1,m)\big\{\widehat{\pi}_{C,a}(m_{j})-\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\big\}\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{n}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\mu(C,1,m)\pi_{C,a}(m_{j})\big\{\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})-\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\big\}\,\mathrm{d}m=o_{p}(1),

which implies

\mathbb{P}_{n}\Big[\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)-\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\pi_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)\Big]=o_{p}(1)

This yields
\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)=o_{p}(1)
. Note that the expressions of
\psi_{j}^{(3)}(\cdot)
and Assumption
4.2
ensure

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(3)}\big(\widehat{e}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(-j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,1,M_{j}}^{(-j)},\widehat{\mu}\big)\Big|\lesssim\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big\{Y_{i}-\widehat{\mu}(C_{i},1,M_{i})\big\}\bigg|=o_{p}(1),

by
\widehat{\mu}(\bm{\cdot})
is correctly estimated, which gives
\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(3)}\big(\widehat{e}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(-j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,1,M_{-j}}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,1}^{(-j)},\widehat{\mu}\big)=o_{p}(1)
. Similarly,

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(1)}\big(\widehat{e}_{0},\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)}\big)\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m_{j},M_{-j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu(C,1,m_{j},M_{-j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle+\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\Big\{\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m_{j},M_{-j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})-\mu(C,1,m_{j},M_{-j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})\Big\}\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}\bigg|.

The first term above is
o_{p}(1)
obviously by the definition of
\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)
. By using the same steps for proving
\mathbb{P}_{n}\Big[\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)-\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\pi_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)\Big]=o_{p}(1)
above, we can prove the second term is also
o_{p}(1)
by both
\widehat{\mu}
and
\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})
are correctly estimated. This yields
\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(1)}\big(\widehat{e}_{0},\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)}\big)=o_{p}(1)
, and similarly gives
\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(2)}\big(\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}},\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)=o_{p}(1)
. The proof for
Part 0
is thus completed.

Part 1.
When the model
\mathscr{M}_{j,\,1}
is correctly specified, we have consistency estimator
\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}
,
\widehat{\mu}
, and
\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)}
.
We first handle
\psi_{j}^{(0)}(\cdot)+\psi_{j}^{(1)}(\cdot)
. We rewrite

\displaystyle\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\pi_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)+\psi_{j}^{(1)}\big({e}_{0},\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)+\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{{e}_{0}(C)}\bigg\{\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu(C,1,m_{j},M_{-j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)}{e_{0}(C)}\big[\mathds{1}(A=0)-e_{0}(C)\big]

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{{e}_{0}(C)}\bigg\{\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu(C,1,m_{j},M_{-j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{P}_{n}\big[\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)+\psi_{j}^{(1)}\big(\widehat{e}_{0},\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)}\big)\big]\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\frac{\int_{\mathcal{M}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)\widehat{\pi}_{C,a}(m_{j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m}{\widehat{e}_{0}(C)}\big\{\mathds{1}(A=0)-\widehat{e}_{0}(C)\big\}\bigg]\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{\widehat{e}_{0}(C)}\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m_{j},M_{-j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg]\Bigg|.

By the bounded assumption in Assumption
7.9
, we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\frac{\int_{\mathcal{M}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)\widehat{\pi}_{C,a}(m_{j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m}{\widehat{e}_{0}(C)}\big\{\mathds{1}(A=0)-\widehat{e}_{0}(C)\big\}\bigg]\Bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{P}_{n}\big\{\mathds{1}(A=0)-\widehat{e}_{0}(C)\big\}\Big|=o_{p}(1)

by
\widehat{e}_{0}(\cdot)
convergences to
{e}_{0}(\cdot)
in
\ell^{2}
norm and
\mathrm{E}\big\{\mathds{1}(A=0)-{e}_{0}(C)\big\}=0
. On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{\widehat{e}_{0}(C)}\bigg\{\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m_{j},M_{-j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by consistency of $\widehat{e}_{0}$, $\widehat{\mu}$, and $\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)}$}}{=}\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{{e}_{0}(C)}\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu(C,1,m_{j},M_{-j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{E}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{{e}_{0}(C)}\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu(C,1,m_{j},M_{-j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by \eqref{strategy_1_DM}}}{=}o_{p}(1),

which gives
\mathbb{P}_{n}\big[\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)+\psi_{j}^{(1)}\big(\widehat{e}_{0},\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)}\big)\big]=o_{p}(1)
. It remains to show
\mathbb{P}_{n}\big[\psi_{j}^{(2)}\big(\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}},\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)+\psi_{j}^{(3)}\big(\widehat{e}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(-j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,1,M_{j}}^{(-j)},\widehat{\mu}\big)\big]=o_{p}(1)
. Indeed, the consistency of
\widehat{\mu}
ensures
\mathbb{P}_{n}\big[\psi_{j}^{(3)}\big(\widehat{e}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(-j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,1,M_{j}}^{(-j)},\widehat{\mu}\big)\big]=o_{p}(1)
as stated in
Part 0
, and

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{P}_{n}\big[\psi_{j}^{(2)}\big(\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}},\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)\big]\Big|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}(C)}\bigg\{\int_{\mathcal{M}_{-j}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,M_{j},m_{-j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{-j}

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathcal{M}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)\widehat{\pi}_{C,1}(m_{j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m\bigg\}\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}(C)}\int_{\mathcal{M}_{-j}}\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{-j}\bigg|\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m_{j},m_{-j})\big[\pi_{C,1}(m_{j})-\widehat{\pi}_{C,1}(m_{j})\big]\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}\bigg|\bigg]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by $\ell^{2}$ assumption and Cauchy}}{\lesssim}\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg|\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\big[\pi_{C,1}(m_{j})-\widehat{\pi}_{C,1}(m_{j})\big]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\mathrm{E}\bigg|\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\big[\pi_{C,1}(m_{j})-\widehat{\pi}_{C,1}(m_{j})\big]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}\bigg|+o_{p}(1)=o_{p}(1),

which completes the proof required for
Part 1
.

Part 2.
Exactly the same as
Part 1
, one can prove when model
\mathscr{M}_{j,\,2}
is correctly specified, we have

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\Big[\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)+\psi_{j}^{(2)}\big(\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}},\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)\Big]\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)\widehat{\pi}_{C,1}(m_{j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}(C)}\bigg\{\int_{\mathcal{M}_{-j}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,M_{j},m_{-j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{-j}-\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg\}\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\frac{\int_{\mathcal{M}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)\widehat{\pi}_{C,a}(m_{j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m}{e_{a^{\prime}}(C)}\big[\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})-\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}(C)\big]\bigg|

\displaystyle+\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}(C)}\int_{\mathcal{M}_{-j}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,M_{j},m_{-j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{-j}-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\big[\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})-\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}(C)\big]^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathrm{E}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{{e}_{a^{\prime}}(C)}\int_{\mathcal{M}_{-j}}\mu(C,1,M_{j},m_{-j})\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{-j}-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by \eqref{strategy_3_DM}}}{=}o_{p}(1)+0+o_{p}(1)=o_{p}(1).

Similarly, we can prove

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(1)}(\widehat{e}_{0},\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)})\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{{e}_{0}(C)}\bigg\{\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}\int_{\mathcal{M}_{-j}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m_{j},m_{-j})\big[\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})-\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\big]\,\mathrm{d}m_{-j}\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg|\int_{\mathcal{M}_{-j}}\big[\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})-\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\big]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}m_{-j}\bigg|=o_{p}(1)

and
\mathbb{P}_{n}\big[\psi_{j}^{(3)}\big(\widehat{e}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(-j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,1,M_{j}}^{(-j)},\widehat{\mu}\big)\big]=o_{p}(1)
,
which completes the proof of this part.

Part 3.
When the model
\mathscr{M}_{j,\,3}
is correctly specified, we have
\widehat{e}_{1}
,
\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(-j)}
, and
\widehat{\pi}_{C,1,M_{j}}^{(-j)}
are consistent, as well as
\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime},\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}^{(j)}
thus
\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)}
, but not
\widehat{\mu}
. In this case, we first rewrite

\displaystyle\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\mu,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\pi_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)+\psi_{j}^{(3)}\big(e_{1},\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(-j)},\pi_{C,1,M_{j}}^{(-j)},\mu\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{e_{1}(C)}\frac{\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(M_{-j})}{\pi_{C,1,M_{j}}(M_{-j})}Y-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{M}}\mu(C,1,m)\pi_{C,1}(m_{j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m-\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{e_{1}(C)}\frac{\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(M_{-j})}{\pi_{C,1,M_{j}}(M_{-j})}\mu(C,1,M).

By using the same empirical processes technique in
Part 0
, we can prove

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\Big[\psi_{j}^{(0)}\big(\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)+\psi_{j}^{(3)}\big(\widehat{e}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(-j)},\widehat{\pi}_{C,1,M_{j}}^{(-j)},\widehat{\mu}\big)\Big]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{\widehat{e}_{1}(C)}\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}(M_{-j})}{\widehat{\pi}_{C,1,M_{j}}(M_{-j})}Y-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle+\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)\widehat{\pi}_{C,1}(m_{j})\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m-\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{\widehat{e}_{1}(C)}\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}(M_{-j})}{\widehat{\pi}_{C,1,M_{j}}(M_{-j})}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,M)\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathrm{E}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{e_{1}(C)}\frac{\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(M_{-j})}{\pi_{C,1,M_{j}}(M_{-j})}Y-\mathrm{E}\zeta_{j}(a^{\prime},0,C)\bigg]+o_{p}(1)\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\mathrm{E}\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)\pi_{C,1}(m_{j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m-\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{e_{1}(C)}\frac{\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(M_{-j})}{\pi_{C,1,M_{j}}(M_{-j})}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,M)\bigg]+o_{p}(1)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by \eqref{strategy_1_DM}}}{=}\Bigg|\mathrm{E}\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)\pi_{C,1}(m_{j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m-\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{e_{1}(C)}\frac{\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(M_{-j})}{\pi_{C,1,M_{j}}(M_{-j})}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,M)\bigg]\Bigg|+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathrm{E}\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)\pi_{C,1}(m_{j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m-\mathrm{E}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{e_{1}(C)}\frac{\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(M_{-j})}{\pi_{C,1,M_{j}}(M_{-j})}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,M)\mid C\bigg]\bigg]\Bigg|+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0+o_{p}(1)=o_{p}(1),

where we use the fact that

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{e_{1}(C)}\frac{\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(M_{-j})}{\pi_{C,1,M_{j}}(M_{-j})}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,M)\mid C\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{M}}\frac{1}{f(a=1\mid C)}\frac{f(m_{-j}\mid C,a=a^{\prime})}{f(m_{-j}\mid C,a=1,m_{j})}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)\mathds{1}(a=1)f(a,m\mid C)\,\mathrm{d}m

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{M}}\frac{1}{f(a=1\mid C)}\frac{f(m_{-j}\mid C,a=a^{\prime})}{f(m_{-j}\mid C,a=1,m_{j})}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)

\displaystyle\times f(a=1\mid C)f(m_{j}\mid C,a=1)f(m_{-j}\mid C,a=1,m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{M}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)f(m_{j}\mid C,a=1)f(m_{-j}\mid C,a=a^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}m=\int_{\mathcal{M}}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,m)\pi_{C,1}(m_{j})\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(m_{-j})\,\mathrm{d}m.

We can also prove that

\Big|\mathbb{P}_{n}\psi_{j}^{(1)}(\widehat{e}_{0},\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{(j)})\Big|\lesssim\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg|\int_{\mathcal{M}_{-j}}\big[\pi_{C,0}(m_{-j})-\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}(m_{-j})\big]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}m_{-j}\bigg|=o_{p}(1)

and

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{P}_{n}\big[\psi_{j}^{(2)}\big(\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}},\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}^{(-j)}\big)\big]\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg|\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\big[\pi_{C,1}(m_{j})-\widehat{\pi}_{C,1}(m_{j})\big]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{n}\bigg|\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\big[\pi_{C,1,\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}(m_{j})-\widehat{\pi}_{C,1,\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}(m_{j})\big]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}\bigg|

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1).

Thus, we finish proof of
Part 3
. The proof of consistency of
\widehat{DM}_{j}^{\text{QR}}
is hence completed.

Semiparametric Efficiency of

\widehat{TM}_{j}^{\text{avg},\,\text{QR}}
:

Next, we want to prove the semiparametric efficiency for
\widehat{TM}_{j}^{\text{avg},\,\text{QR}}
. Here we have all estimators in model
\mathscr{M}_{j,\,\ell},\ell=0,1,2,3
are correct. We use
\widehat{P}
to denote these correct estimated functions or estimated distribution, which is a consistent estimator of the true law of
X
,
P
. First note that, for any fixed
\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\widehat{\mathcal{C}}_{M})
and
j\in[p]
,
\widehat{TM}_{j}^{\text{QR}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})
can be written as the following one-step estimators

\displaystyle\widehat{TM}_{j}^{\text{QR}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{n}\Bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{\widehat{e}_{1}(C)}\Big\{Y-\widehat{\mu}(C,1)\Big\}-\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{\widehat{e}_{0}(C)}\Big\{Y-\widehat{\mu}(C,0)\big]\Big\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\mathbb{P}_{n}\Bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{\widehat{e}_{1}(C)}\widehat{\pi}_{C,1}\big(\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\{Y-\widehat{\mu}(C,1,\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),M_{j})\Big\}

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{\widehat{e}_{0}(C)}\widehat{\pi}_{C,0}\big(\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\{Y-\widehat{\mu}(C,0,\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),M_{j})\Big\}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{\widehat{e}_{1}(C)}\widehat{\mu}(C,1,\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),m_{j})-\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{\widehat{e}_{0}(C)}\widehat{\mu}(C,0,\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),m_{j})\bigg)\widehat{\pi}_{C}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}

\displaystyle-\bigg(\frac{\mathds{1}(A=1)}{\widehat{e}_{1}(C)}\widehat{\mathrm{E}}\big[\varrho_{j}(1,M_{j},C\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\mid C\big]-\frac{\mathds{1}(A=0)}{\widehat{e}_{0}(C)}\widehat{\mathrm{E}}\big[\varrho_{j}(0,M_{j},C\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\mid C\big]\bigg)\Bigg]+\widehat{TM}_{j}^{\mathscr{M}_{0}}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\Big[\mathbb{P}_{n}\upvarphi_{j,1}^{\text{QR}}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})+\uppsi_{j,1}(\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\Big]-\Big[\mathbb{P}_{n}\upvarphi_{j,0}^{\text{QR}}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})+\uppsi_{j,0}(\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\Big]

where
\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}
with
a^{\prime}=0,1
is defined as

\displaystyle\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}:F_{X}\times\mathcal{G}_{M}\,\mapsto\,\mathrm{E}\Bigg[

\displaystyle\bigg\{\mu(a^{\prime},C)-\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\mu(C,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j},M_{j})\,\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(\operatorname{pa}_{j})\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}\bigg\}\Bigg].

and
\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}
is the mapping from the observation and the underlying distribution of
X
to the efficient score for
\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}
, from Theorem
4.3
, we know that

\displaystyle\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{\text{QR}}(X,Q\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(C)}\Big\{Y-\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},C)\Big\}+\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},C)-\Bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(C)}\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\{Y-\mu^{Q}(C,a^{\prime},M)\Big\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle-\Bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(C)}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu^{Q}(C,a^{\prime},\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),m_{j})\pi_{C}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\mathrm{E}_{P}\big[\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},M_{j},C\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\mid C\big]\right\}

\displaystyle+\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},M_{j},C\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\Bigg]-\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}(Q\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(1)}(X,Q\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(2)}(X,Q\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}(Q\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})

for arbitrary distribution
Q
for
X
, where
e^{Q}_{a^{\prime}}(c):=Q(A=a^{\prime}\mid c)
and similar definition for
k^{Q}(a^{\prime},c)
,
\pi_{S}^{Q}(m_{T})
,
\mu^{Q}(\cdot)
, and
\rho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},m_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})
, where

\displaystyle\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(1)}(X,Q\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(C)}\Big\{Y-\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},C)\Big\}+\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},C)-\Bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(C)}\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\{Y-\mu^{Q}(C,a^{\prime},M)\Big\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},M_{j},C\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M}),

and

\displaystyle\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(2)}(X,Q\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathds{1}(A=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(C)}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu^{Q}(C,a^{\prime},\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),m_{j})\pi_{C}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\mathrm{E}_{P}\big[\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},M_{j},C\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\mid C\big]\right\}.

We can see that
\uppsi_{j,1}-\uppsi_{j,0}
the functional mapping of the underlying distribution of
X
to
TM_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})
for a fixed DAG
\mathcal{G}_{M}
. Therefore, we can decompose

\displaystyle\widehat{TM}_{j}^{\text{QR}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})-TM_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\langle\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P\right)\upvarphi_{j,\bm{\cdot}}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})+\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P\right)\big[\upvarphi_{j,\bm{\cdot}}^{\text{QR}}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,\bm{\cdot}}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\big]+R_{j,\bm{\cdot}}^{\text{QR}}(\widehat{P},{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\Big\rangle

where

R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{\text{QR}}(\widehat{P},{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})=\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}(\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}({P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})+\int\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{\text{QR}}(x,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\,\mathrm{d}P(x).

Given Assumption
7.4
, by using the argument in Proof of Theorem
7.8
, we can prove

\displaystyle\widehat{TM}_{j}^{\text{avg},\,\text{QR}}-\overline{TM}_{j}

(C.7)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\#\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\sum_{\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\big\{\upvarphi_{j,1}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,0}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\#\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\sum_{\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P\right)\Big[\{\upvarphi_{j,1}^{\text{QR}}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,1}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\}

\displaystyle-\{\upvarphi_{j,0}^{\text{QR}}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,0}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\#\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\sum_{\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\big\{R_{j,1}^{\text{QR}}(\widehat{P},{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-R_{j,0}^{\text{QR}}(\widehat{P},{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\big\}+o_{p}(\ell_{n}^{-1})

with any
\ell_{n}
such that
\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\ell_{n}=\infty
.
The (vector-)function classes for
\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})
with
a^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}
is
q=(q_{k})_{k\in\mathcal{K}}\in\mathcal{Q}:=\bigotimes_{\{T,S\}\text{ used in \eqref{DM_MR} and \eqref{IM_MR} for a fixed $j\in[p]$}}\penalty\mathcal{E}\otimes\mathcal{F}_{T\,\mid\,S}\otimes\mathcal{U}_{S}
, which is also VC-class with index the same index
\vartheta_{j}\in[0,1/2)
by Lemma 2.6.18 in
Van Der Vaart et al. 1996
.
On the other hand, note that the consistency in Assumption
7.10
ensures there exists sufficient large
n
such that all denominators in
\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{\text{QR}}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})
will be larger than
\varepsilon/2
, then by using
|a_{1}a_{2}-b_{1}b_{2}|\leq|b_{1}||a_{2}-b_{2}|+|b_{2}||a_{1}-b_{1}|+|a_{1}-a_{2}||b_{1}-b_{2}|
for any real number
a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}
, we can show that

\displaystyle\Big|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\big\{\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{\text{QR}}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\big\}\Big|

(C.8)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by $\ell^{2}$ assumption}}{\lesssim}\underbrace{\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}}\Big|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\big\{\widehat{q}_{k}(X;\mathcal{G}_{M})-{q}_{k}(X;\mathcal{G}_{M})\big\}\Big|}_{\text{comes from }\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(1)}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(1)}(X,{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\Bigg|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big[\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big]\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\Bigg|}_{\text{comes from }\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(2)}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(2)}(X,{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\Bigg|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big[\widehat{\mu}(C,a,\operatorname{pa}_{j},M_{j})-\mu(C,a,\operatorname{pa}_{j},M_{j})\Big]\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}\Bigg|}_{\text{comes from }\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(2)}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(2)}(X,{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\Bigg|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big[\widehat{\pi}_{C,a}(\operatorname{pa}_{j})-\pi_{C,a}(\operatorname{pa}_{j})\Big]\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}\Bigg|}_{\text{comes from }\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(2)}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(2)}(X,{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\Big|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\big[\widehat{\mu}(a^{\prime},C)-\mu(a^{\prime},C)\big]\Big|}_{\text{comes from }\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}(\widehat{P};\mathcal{G}_{M})-\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}({P};\mathcal{G}_{M})}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\Bigg|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big[\widehat{\mu}(C,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j},M_{j})-\mu(C,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j},M_{j})\Big]\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}\bigg]\Bigg|}_{\text{comes from }\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}(\widehat{P};\mathcal{G}_{M})-\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}({P};\mathcal{G}_{M})}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\Bigg|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big[\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}(\operatorname{pa}_{j})-\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(\operatorname{pa}_{j})\Big]\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}\bigg]\Bigg|}_{\text{comes from }\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}(\widehat{P};\mathcal{G}_{M})-\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}({P};\mathcal{G}_{M})}.

For any
k\in\mathcal{K}
,
\widehat{q}_{k}
converge with
\ell^{2}
-norm to
q_{k}
at a rate of
n^{-\vartheta_{q_{k}}^{*}}
in class
\mathcal{Q}
with
\vartheta_{q_{k}}^{*}\in\{\vartheta_{j,e}^{*},\vartheta_{j,\pi}^{*},\vartheta_{j,\mu}^{*}\}
. Then by Corollary 5.1 in
Chernozhukov et al. 2014
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\Big|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\big\{\widehat{q}_{k}(X;\mathcal{G}_{M})-{q}_{k}(X;\mathcal{G}_{M})\big\}\Big|

\displaystyle\asymp

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sqrt{n^{\vartheta_{j}}}n^{-\vartheta_{q_{k}}^{*}}\big(\log n^{-\vartheta_{q_{k}}^{*}}+\log n\big)

\displaystyle\asymp

\displaystyle O(n^{-1/2+\vartheta_{j}/2-\vartheta_{q_{k}}^{*}}\log n)=o\big(n^{-1/2}\big)

by
\vartheta_{j}/2-\min\{\vartheta_{j,e}^{*},\vartheta_{j,\pi}^{*},\vartheta_{j,\mu}^{*}\}<0
in Assumption
7.10
,
which implies
(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\big\{\widehat{q}_{k}(X;\mathcal{G}_{M})-{q}_{k}(X;\mathcal{G}_{M})\big\}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2})
for any
\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})
. Thus,

\displaystyle(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\big[\widehat{\mu}(a^{\prime},C)-\mu(a^{\prime},C)\big]=o_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big[\widehat{\mu}(C,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j},M_{j})-\mu(C,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j},M_{j})\Big]\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}\bigg]=o_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big[\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}(\operatorname{pa}_{j})-\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}(\operatorname{pa}_{j})\Big]\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2}).

Similarly, for any
\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})
we can prove

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\Bigg|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big[\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big]\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\Bigg|=o(n^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\Bigg|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big[\widehat{\mu}(C,a,\operatorname{pa}_{j},M_{j})-\mu(C,a,\operatorname{pa}_{j},M_{j})\Big]\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}\Bigg|=o(n^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\Bigg|(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big[\widehat{\pi}_{C,a}(\operatorname{pa}_{j})-\pi_{C,a}(\operatorname{pa}_{j})\Big]\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}\Bigg|=o(n^{-1/2}).

Therefore, we conclude

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\#\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\sum_{\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\Big[\{\upvarphi_{j,1}^{\text{QR}}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,1}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\}\Big]=o_{p}\big(n^{-1/2}\big),

(C.9)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\#\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\sum_{\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P)\{\upvarphi_{j,0}^{\text{QR}}(X,\widehat{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,0}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\}=o_{p}\big(n^{-1/2}\big).

It remains to deal with the reminder
R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{\text{QR}}(\widehat{P},P)
for
a^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}
. Note that for any distribution
Q
, we rewrite

\displaystyle R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{\text{QR}}(Q,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}(Q\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}(P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})+\int\upvarphi_{j,a^{\prime}}^{\text{QR}}(x,Q\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\uppsi_{j,a^{\prime}}(P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})+\int\Bigg\{\frac{\mathds{1}(a=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\Big\{y-\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},c)\Big\}+\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},c)

\displaystyle-\Bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(a=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\pi_{C,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{Pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\{y-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathds{1}(a=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\left(\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),m_{j})\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\mathrm{E}_{P}\big[\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},M_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\mid c\big]\right)

\displaystyle+\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},m_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\Bigg]\Bigg\}\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle=-\int\Big[\kappa(a^{\prime},c)-\varrho_{j}(a^{\prime},m_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\Big]\,\mathrm{d}P(x)+\int\Bigg\{\frac{\mathds{1}(a=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\Big\{y-\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},c)\Big\}+\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},c)

\displaystyle-\Bigg[\frac{\mathds{1}(a=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\frac{\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})}{\pi_{c,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}^{Q}(m_{j})}\Big\{y-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathds{1}(a=a^{\prime})}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\left(\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),m_{j})\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\mathrm{E}_{P}\big[\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},M_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\mid c\big]\right)

\displaystyle+\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},m_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\Bigg]\Bigg\}\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by rearranging}}{=}\int\Bigg\{\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\Big\{\kappa(a^{\prime},c)-\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},c)\Big\}+\Big\{\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},c)-\kappa(a^{\prime},c)\Big\}

\displaystyle-\Bigg[\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\frac{\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})}{\pi_{c,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}^{Q}(m_{j})}\Big\{\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\left(\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),m_{j})\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\mathrm{E}_{P}\big[\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},M_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\mid c\big]\right)

\displaystyle+\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},m_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\varrho_{j}(a^{\prime},m_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\Bigg]\Bigg\}\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(1)}(Q,P)+R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(2)}(Q,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(3)}(Q,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})+R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(4)}(Q,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M}).

In the equation, we use the identities

\displaystyle\int\bigg[\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\frac{\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})}{\pi_{c,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}^{Q}(m_{j})}\Big\{\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\}

\displaystyle+\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},m_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\varrho_{j}(a^{\prime},m_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\bigg]\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{expanding the formula of }\varrho_{j}^{Q}}{=}\int\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\Bigg[\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\{\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\}

\displaystyle-\int\bigg[\Big\{\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\}\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)

\displaystyle+\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\{\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\}\bigg]\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{rearranging}}{=}\int\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\bigg[\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}-1\bigg]\Big\{\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\}\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle-\int\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\{\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\}\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),

and

\displaystyle\int\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\{\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\}\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle+\int\,\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\left(\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),m_{j})\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}-\mathrm{E}_{P}\big[\varrho_{j}^{Q}(a^{\prime},M_{j},c\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\mid c\big]\right)\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{expanding the expectation}}{=}\int\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\int\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\{\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\}\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle-\int\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big\{{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\}\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{rearranging}}{=}\int\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\int\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\{\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\}\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle-\int\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big\{{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\}\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle+\int\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\Big\{\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\}\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big\{{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\}\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{rearranging}}{=}-\int\left(\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}-1\right)\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big\{{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big\}\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle+\int\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big({\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big)\Big(\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big)\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),

and then the components
R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(\ell)}(\bm{\cdot})
with
\ell=1,2,3,4
can be rewritten as following

\displaystyle R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(1)}(Q,P)

\displaystyle=\int\Bigg[\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\Big\{\kappa(a^{\prime},c)-\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},c)\Big\}+\Big\{\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},c)-\kappa(a^{\prime},c)\Big\}\Bigg]\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle=\int\bigg(\frac{1}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}-\frac{1}{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}\bigg)\Big(\kappa(a^{\prime},c)-\kappa^{Q}(a^{\prime},c)\Big)e_{a^{\prime}}(c)\,\mathrm{d}P(x),

\displaystyle R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(2)}(Q,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\bigg[\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}-1\bigg]\Big\{\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big\}\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\bigg(\frac{1}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}-\frac{1}{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}\bigg)\Big(\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big)e_{a^{\prime}}(c)\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\,\mathrm{d}P(x),

and

\displaystyle R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(3)}(Q,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle=\int e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\int_{\mathcal{M}_{j}}\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}m_{j}

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\bigg(\frac{1}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}-\frac{1}{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}\bigg)\Big({\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big)\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}),

\displaystyle R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(4)}(Q,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle=\int\frac{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}{e_{a^{\prime}}^{Q}(c)}\pi_{c}^{Q}(m_{j})\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{M}_{\operatorname{pa}_{j}}(\mathcal{G}_{M})}\Big({\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big)\Big(\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\mu^{Q}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big)\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M}).

Therefore, by Assumption
7.10
, we have for sufficient large
n
such that

\Big|\widehat{f}(x_{S_{2}}\mid x_{S_{1}})-{f}(x_{S_{2}}\mid x_{S_{1}})\Big|\leq{f}(x_{S_{2}}\mid x_{S_{1}})\quad\text{and}\quad\Big|\widehat{\mathrm{E}}\big[x_{S_{2}}\mid x_{S_{1}}\big]-{\mathrm{E}}\big[x_{S_{2}}\mid x_{S_{1}}\big]\Big|\leq{\mathrm{E}}\big[x_{S_{2}}\mid x_{S_{1}}\big]

almost surely. Then components
R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(\ell)}(\bm{\cdot})
with
\ell=1,2,3,4
can be furthermore bounded by

\displaystyle\big|R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(1)}(\widehat{P},P)\big|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\int\bigg(\frac{1}{\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}(c)}-\frac{1}{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}\bigg)\Big(\kappa(a^{\prime},c)-\widehat{\kappa}(a^{\prime},c)\Big)e_{a^{\prime}}(c)\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by Assumption \ref{ass_pos}}}{\leq}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int\big|e_{a^{\prime}}(c)-\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}(c)\big|\big|\kappa(a^{\prime},c)-\widehat{\kappa}(a^{\prime},c)\big|\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by Assumption \ref{ass_conv_rate_QR}}}{\asymp}n^{-\vartheta_{j,e}^{*}+\vartheta_{j,\mu}^{*}}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

and similarly

\displaystyle\big|R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(2)}(\widehat{P},P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\big|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\int\bigg(\frac{1}{\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}(c)}-\frac{1}{e_{a^{\prime}}(c)}\bigg)\Big(\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\widehat{\mu}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big)e_{a^{\prime}}(c)\pi_{c,a^{\prime}}^{Q}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\,\mathrm{d}\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\,\mathrm{d}P(x)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by Assumption \ref{ass_pos}}}{\lesssim}\int\big|e_{a^{\prime}}(c)-\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}(c)\big|\big|\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\widehat{\mu}(c,a^{\prime},m)\big|\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by Assumption \ref{ass_conv_rate_QR}}}{\asymp}n^{-\vartheta_{j,e}^{*}+\vartheta_{j,\mu}^{*}}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle\big|R_{j,a^{\prime}}^{(3)}(\widehat{P},P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\big|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by $\ell^{2}$ assmuption and Assumption \ref{ass_pos}}}{\lesssim}\int\big|e_{a^{\prime}}(c)-\widehat{e}_{a^{\prime}}(c)\big|\Big|{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big|\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by Assumption \ref{ass_conv_rate_QR}}}{\asymp}n^{-\vartheta_{j,e}^{*}+\vartheta_{j,\pi}^{*}}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

and

\displaystyle\big|R_{a^{\prime}}^{(4)}(\widehat{P},P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\big|

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by $\ell^{2}$ assmuption and Assumption \ref{ass_pos}}}{\lesssim}\int\Big|{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)-\widehat{\pi}_{C,a^{\prime}}\big(\operatorname{pa}_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\Big|\Big|\mu(c,a^{\prime},m)-\widehat{\mu}(c,a^{\prime},m)\Big|\,\mathrm{d}P(x)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by Assumption \ref{ass_conv_rate_QR}}}{\asymp}n^{-\vartheta_{j,\pi}^{*}+\vartheta_{j,\mu}^{*}}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2}).

Therefore, for the reminder, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\#\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\sum_{\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}R_{j,1}^{\text{QR}}(\widehat{P},{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})

(C.10)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\#\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\sum_{\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}R_{j,0}^{\text{QR}}(\widehat{P},{P}\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})

\displaystyle=o_{p}\big(n^{-1/2}\big)

for our quadruply robust estimators. Plug (
C.9
) and (
C.10
) into (
C.7
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\big(\widehat{TM}_{j}^{\text{avg},\,\text{QR}}-\overline{TM}_{j}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\#\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\sum_{\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\Big[\mathbb{G}_{n}\big\{\upvarphi_{j,1}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,0}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\big\}+o_{p}(1)\Big]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by $o_{p}(1)$ is uniform on $\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})$}}{=}\mathbb{G}_{n}\Bigg\{\frac{1}{\#\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\sum_{\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{M})}\big\{\upvarphi_{j,1}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})-\upvarphi_{j,0}^{\text{QR}}(X,P\,;\,\mathcal{G}_{M})\big\}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\rightsquigarrow\,\mathcal{N}\left(0,\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{\#\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{0,M})}\sum_{\mathcal{G}_{M}\in\operatorname{MEC}(\mathcal{C}_{0,M})}S^{\text{eff},\,\text{nonpar}}\big(TM_{j}(\mathcal{G}_{M})\big)\right]^{2}\right)

which completes the proof of the semiparametric efficiency for
\widehat{TM}_{j}^{\text{avg},\,\text{QR}}
.
∎
